### [1] [How Should Diffusion Language Models Edit Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38257)
*Xijia Tao,Ziru Liu,Shansan Gong,Jiacheng Ye,Kecheng Chen,Zirui Wu,Lin Zheng,Xinyu Fu,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 扩散模型具备强填充能力但难以预测编辑位置；编辑接口必须同时暴露所有必需改动并限制不必要的重生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码编辑既要决定修改位置，又要生成新内容并保留未改动部分。论文旨在理解扩散语言模型如何在不同接口下分配“哪里改”和“如何改”的职责，以及为什么强大的填充能力并不等同于可靠的编辑能力。

Method: 比较四种编辑接口（整文件重写、查找并替换、先定位再填充、逐标记编辑），在CanItEdit代码编辑基准上评估扩散模型。通过固定生成模型与解码流程，改变可编辑区域来分析不同接口对定位与生成职责的影响，并用句级Wiki编辑作为跨领域探针验证发现。

Result: 发现“组合缺口”：尽管模型可在给定位置时完成复杂协调改动，但在需要预测位置时性能大幅下降。保留原始代码有助于在多个区域内填充，但不能解决位置选择问题。成功编辑需要两项能力：覆盖每个必要改动与在边界上精确定位；遗漏区域会导致改动缺失，而为保证覆盖而扩大区域会强制重生成不变代码，显著降低成功率。这一现象在Wiki句子编辑中也得到验证。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在获知确切编辑位置时能生成协调一致的改动，但在必须预测这些位置时大多能力丧失，形成“组合缺口”。要成功编辑，接口必须同时保证覆盖所有必要改动并为每个改动划定精确边界。

Abstract: Code editing requires a model to decide where to make changes, generate the new content, and preserve everything else. We study how masked diffusion language models divide these responsibilities across four editing interfaces: whole-file rewriting, search-and-replace, locate-then-infill, and token-level editing. Experiments on CanItEdit reveal a composition gap: diffusion models can generate coordinated changes when the correct edit locations are supplied, but much of this capability is lost when those locations must be predicted. Access to the intact original code helps the model fill multiple edit regions, yet does not resolve the difficulty of selecting those regions. By varying the editable regions while holding the generation model and decoding procedure fixed, we identify two distinct requirements for successful editing: covering every required change and placing precise boundaries around it. Missing a required region prevents the corresponding change, while widening regions to ensure coverage can sharply reduce success by requiring unchanged code to be regenerated. A sentence-level Wiki editing probe shows the same qualitative gap between supplied and predicted locations beyond code. These findings show why strong infilling capability alone does not ensure reliable editing: the interface must expose all required changes while limiting regeneration of unchanged code.

</details>


### [2] [Zero2Repo: Can Coding Agents Build Repositories from Scratch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38269)
*Pei Yang,Tianyu Shi,Yuhang Yao,Wanyi Chen,Tongyun Yang,Dun Pei,Haonan Wang,Pengbin Feng,Guanxu Yu,Jingchun Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Shuhan Sun,Hao Li,Xiang Li,Jie Xiao,Xinyu Wang,Hanxin Chen,Daqi Li,Qi Jia,Hongshan Lin,Zhizhou Gu,Zijun Tian,Weizhi Du,Lynn Ai,Eric Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Zero2Repo 用自动化流水线将真实开源项目转成可执行的端到端构建任务，以严格的执行验收与多语言支持揭示现有代码生成代理在完整仓库构建上的短板，并提供可定位的失败信号便于改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从零构建代码库的评测多为单语言且任务手工编写，不足以衡量代理在真实多语言生态与完工交付（end-to-end repository construction）上的能力。

Method: 通过自动化 authoring pipeline 将真实、版本固定的开源项目转为行为规范、可复现运行环境与隐藏的验收测试；对每个任务进行执行级验证（参考实现必须通过，且对抗性验证要拒绝错误实现）；在隔离容器中运行代理，提交前隐藏测试，采用二元通过奖励，无 LLM 判定，且方法不依赖语言特定假设。

Result: 当前发布包含 Python、TypeScript、Go、C++ 任务。即使在 11 个可能已见过的仓库任务上，最强代理也仅解决 10 个；失败提交通常仍通过 90–99% 的隐藏测试，且大多数失败可归因于单一遗漏或低频规则，为针对性改进提供明确目标。

Conclusion: Zero2Repo 提供了一个语言无关、基于执行验证的从零构建代码库的基准，展示了现代代码生成代理在真实、多语言、完整项目构建任务上的显著挑战，并将失败定位为可修正的具体缺陷。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly asked to build software rather than patch it, yet benchmarks for from-scratch repository construction are mostly limited to a single language and depend on manually curated tasks. We introduce Zero2Repo, a benchmark in which an agent receives a product requirements document, an interface contract, and an empty workspace, and must deliver a complete repository in the project's native ecosystem. Tasks are produced by a language-agnostic authoring pipeline that converts real, version-pinned open-source projects into behavioral specifications, reproducible environments, and hidden acceptance tests. Each task is validated by execution: a reference implementation derived from the upstream project must pass, and adversarial validation must show that the tests reject incorrect implementations. Evaluation runs production coding agents in isolated containers, withholds the acceptance tests until an explicit submission, and assigns a binary reward only when every test passes, with no LLM judge. The pipeline and harness make no language-specific assumptions and apply to mainstream programming ecosystems; the current release contains Python, TypeScript, Go, and C++ tasks. Even on 11 tasks drawn from repositories that frontier models have very likely seen during training, the strongest agent solves only 10, and every failing submission passes 90-99% of the hidden tests; for the two strongest agents, 67-100% of failed tests trace to a single omission or a low-frequency rule stated in the specification rather than to a missing subsystem, so each failure is a concrete target for improvement.

</details>


### [3] [E2E-SWE: Benchmarking LLMs on Building Working Codebases from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38335)
*Hantian Ding,Chloe Bi,Jiacheng Zhu,John Yang,Matt Deitke,Pengcheng Yin,Zijian Wang,Rui Hou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: E2E-SWE是针对整仓生成的严格基准（186题，11种语言），通过工程师+LLM设计与自治代理迭代验证确保可解性；在此基准上13模型表现差异大，揭示生成完整项目的高复杂性与对系统级规划的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的编程代理从局部代码修改扩展到生成完整软件仓库，急需一个能考察系统级推理、且规范精确且实现无关的评测基准，以衡量端到端从空工作区到可安装项目的生成能力。

Method: 构建186个完整仓库生成任务：由软件工程师与LLM联合设计实现无关的自然语言规范和综合隐藏测试；通过自治代理对任务进行迭代验证与修复（静态检查 + 模型运行失败反馈），确保任务可解且规范明确。使用该基准评估了13个前沿模型，统计通过率并分析代理生成轨迹。

Result: E2E-SWE提供了严格可复现的端到端评测，显示模型间存在显著差异（pass@1 11.7%–67.7%），大多数模型仍有显著提升空间；轨迹分析表明生成过程需要长且前置的规划推理。

Conclusion: 论文提出了面向端到端仓库生成的基准E2E-SWE，并通过工程师与LLM协作构建186个跨11门语言的整仓任务，展示了在严格、实现无关的规范与隐藏测试下对模型能力的强区分性（pass@1在11.7%至67.7%之间），同时揭示模型在构建完整代码库时依赖的前置规划与系统级推理。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are evolving from making localized code changes to developing complete software repositories. However, evaluating repository-scale generation remains challenging: tasks must demand system-level reasoning while ensuring that all evaluated behaviors are precisely specified and independent of any particular implementation. We introduce E2E-SWE, a benchmark for evaluating whether coding agents can build complete, functional software repositories end to end. E2E-SWE contains 186 whole-repository generation tasks spanning 11 programming languages. Given only a natural-language specification and an empty workspace, an agent must implement a complete, installable project that satisfies a comprehensive suite of hidden tests. Each task is constructed by a software engineer in collaboration with an LLM; together, they develop the test suite and a corresponding implementation-independent specification. To ensure that tasks are well specified and practically solvable, we further subject them to an iterative verification process in which autonomous agents audit and repair task defects using static inspection and failures observed from real model rollouts. Evaluating 13 frontier models, we find substantial variation in end-to-end repository generation ability, with pass@1 ranging from 11.7% to 67.7%, providing strong model differentiation while leaving considerable headroom for future progress. Analysis of agent trajectories further reveals long, front-loaded reasoning patterns, highlighting the planning and system-level reasoning required to construct working codebases from scratch.

</details>


### [4] [OpenCollab: A Multi-Agent Coding Framework with Programmable Collaboration and Controllable Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38345)
*Chun-Wah Hsu,Kai Gong,Yu Wu,Xianhe Chen,Mengyang Liu,Jie Li,Hanyu Li,Zhixuan Liu,Naisheng Tang,Jiaying Chi,Ziheng Fan,Xuning He,Xiaokang Yang,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpenCollab 提供可编程组织、受控运行时与细粒度追踪，并通过 Adherence 指标和基准实验表明：组织设计显著影响代理协作行为与性能；在若干任务上，两编码者工作流达成新 SOTA，单代理则在令牌效率上最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多代理代码系统评估通常假定配置的组织被忠实遵守，但现实中代理行为会偏离该假设；此外不同系统组件差异使得无法明确归因性能提升。需要一个统一、可控、可追踪的基础设施来消除这些混淆，支持因果性评估与可重复实验。

Method: 构建一个支持可编程组织（组织模板/角色/通信规则）、共享且可控的运行时环境，并记录细粒度事件流以追踪执行；提出 Adherence 指标以量化宣称的组织是否被实际遵循；通过系统化变量干预（改变单一维度）和在多套编码基准上与现有主流 harness（Mini-SWE-agent、Codex CLI、Claude Code）比较，评估行为和性能差异。

Result: 定义 Adherence 并通过实验发现：改变任一单一配置维度会将 Adherence 从 47.2% 改变到 97.2%；在多代理编码基准上，两编码者工作流基于 OpenCollab 在若干任务上超过 Mini-SWE-agent、Codex CLI 和 Claude Code 等主流方案；单代理配置在所有评估套件中使用的令牌最少；总体上 OpenCollab 提供了便于可编程协作与受控因果评估的统一基础设施。

Conclusion: OpenCollab 提供了一个统一的多代理代码协作框架，通过可编程的组织设计和可控的运行时，以及细粒度事件流跟踪，使得对代理组织行为的归因和可重复评估成为可能。实验证明：组织设计对实际协作行为（Adherence）和最终性能影响极大；在某些设置下，两编码者工作流在若干基准上实现了新的 SOTA，而单代理配置在令牌消耗上最优。

Abstract: Multi-agent coding systems are designed to tackle complex software engineering tasks through collaboration. However, existing evaluations typically assume configured organizations are followed faithfully, whereas reality differs. This behavioral gap, combined with differences in underlying system components, prevents clear attribution of observed gains. To this end, we introduce OpenCollab, a multi-agent coding framework that provides a unified infrastructure for programmable collaboration and controllable runtime. Specifically, OpenCollab unifies organization design, enforces experimental control on a shared runtime, and tracks execution through fine-grained event streams. On this basis, we define Adherence to quantify whether the declared organization is actually realized. Our experiments reveal that agents collaborate very differently across configurations: changing any single dimension shifts Adherence, from 47.2% to as high as 97.2%. Furthermore, extensive agentic coding benchmarks show that a two-coder workflow built on OpenCollab establishes new SOTA performance compared to the mainstream harnesses such as Mini-SWE-agent, Codex CLI, and Claude Code, showing that a well-designed organization can outperform strong existing harnesses, while OpenCollab's single-agent configuration uses the fewest tokens across all evaluated suites. OpenCollab establishes a unified multi-agent infrastructure for easy programmable collaboration and controlled causal evaluation.

</details>


### [5] [From Codebase to Culprit (C2C): Reducing the Search Space for Bugs with Semantic Retrieval and Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38402)
*Ankur Garg,Corey Yang-Smith,Rishav Rishav,Ahmad Abdellatif,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: C2C: contrastive-fine-tuned semantic retrieval + hierarchical RL for multi-level bug localization; outperforms single-granularity baselines on Java/Python; ablations validate key components.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Developers debug top-down (files→functions→lines); existing methods often operate at single granularity and miss efficiency and context preservation. C2C aims to mirror developer workflows and progressively reduce the search space while keeping contextual consistency.

Method: Two-stage approach: (1) retrieval via CodeBERT embeddings fine-tuned with contrastive learning to perform semantic vector similarity search using bug report text and stack traces; (2) Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning that incrementally localizes bugs across file→function→line granularities with structured learning signals and reward shaping to maintain contextual consistency across decisions.

Result: Empirical results show improved retrieval precision and localization accuracy on real-world Java and Python datasets. Ablations show hierarchical decomposition, structured signals, and reward shaping each contribute significantly. The framework enables multi-resolution localization and outperforms single-granularity baselines.

Conclusion: C2C is an effective multi-granularity bug localization framework that combines recall-oriented semantic retrieval with a hierarchical reinforcement-learning pipeline to narrow search from files to functions to lines, yielding improved retrieval precision and localization accuracy on Java and Python datasets.

Abstract: We introduce C2C (From Codebase to Culprit), a framework for precise bug localization that progressively reduces the debugging search space across multiple levels of granularity: files, functions, and lines of code. To mirror developer's natural top-down debugging workflows, C2C integrates semantic retrieval and Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) in a two-stage process. First, it performs recall-oriented retrieval of buggy candidates via semantic vector similarity search using bug-report text, including available stack-trace information, against a database of embeddings, where the embeddings are fine-tuned via contrastive learning with CodeBERT. Building on this reduced search space, the HRL framework incrementally localizes bugs, reasoning from files to functions and ultimately to individual lines of code. Unlike prior approaches which operate at a single granularity, C2C enables multi-resolution localization while maintaining contextual consistency across decisions. Experiments on real-world Java and Python datasets demonstrate that C2C improves retrieval precision and localization accuracy. Ablation studies further highlight the contributions of hierarchical decomposition, structured learning signals, and reward shaping in advancing multi-level bug localization.

</details>


### [6] [MallocSan: A Memory Safety Tool for Native Closed-Source Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38499)
*Adel Belkhiri,Michel Dagenais,Maxime Lamothe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MallocSan provides an LD_PRELOAD-based heap sanitizer for x86-64 Linux binaries using noncanonical-pointer traps and runtime patching, delivering deterministic detection with modest overhead (~4.8x) and good practical coverage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Memory-corruption bugs in C/C++ remain a primary source of critical vulnerabilities, but existing deterministic sanitizers are hard to deploy for closed-source binaries, require hardware support, or impose large runtime costs. There is a need for a deployable, efficient sanitizer that works on unmodified x86-64 Linux binaries.

Method: Uses LD_PRELOAD to interpose allocation APIs; encodes an object identifier into the unused high bits of protected pointers to create noncanonical pointers that fault on dereference; on fault, decodes the pointer and dynamically patches the offending instruction so subsequent executions run userspace per-object bounds checks. Fallbacks include in-handler emulation or single-stepping, and an optional profile-guided offline rewrite further reduces residual overhead. Extends checking to vector gather/scatter and supports policy-scoped coverage.

Result: On seven SPEC CPU2017 benchmarks, MallocSan achieves a geometric-mean slowdown of 4.82x versus native (better than Memcheck's 18.55x) and outperforms Memcheck on five benchmarks. Retains parallel scaling on multithreaded workloads evaluated. On Juliet tests, it detects all seeded heap violations with no false positives on good runs. Also finds real-world heap-buffer errors, including a known LibTIFF overread.

Conclusion: MallocSan demonstrates that a practical, deterministic heap sanitizer for native x86-64 Linux binaries can be implemented without source access, recompilation, or specialized hardware, achieving substantially lower overhead than Valgrind Memcheck while maintaining detection capability and parallel scalability.

Abstract: Memory-corruption errors remain a leading cause of high-impact vulnerabilities in C and C++ software. Deterministic detection, however, remains difficult to deploy: compiler-based sanitizers require source code and control of the build pipeline, hardware-assisted schemes depend on specific platforms, and dynamic binary translation can impose order-of-magnitude slowdowns. This paper presents MallocSan, a heap sanitizer for native, potentially closed-source x86-64 Linux applications that requires no source access, recompilation, or specialized hardware. MallocSan interposes on memory allocation through LD_PRELOAD and embeds an object identifier in the unused high bits of each protected pointer. Dereferencing the resulting noncanonical pointer faults at the offending instruction, which MallocSan decodes and patches at runtime so that subsequent executions perform per-object bounds checks entirely in userspace. Sites that cannot be patched fall back to in-handler emulation or single-stepping, while an optional profile-guided pass rewrites frequently executed residual sites offline. MallocSan also extends identity-based checking to vector gather/scatter instructions and supports policy-scoped coverage. Across seven SPEC CPU 2017 benchmarks, MallocSan outperforms Valgrind Memcheck on five, with a geometric-mean execution-time factor of 4.82x relative to native execution, compared with 18.55x for Memcheck, while preserving substantial parallel scaling on both evaluated multithreaded workloads: 644.nab_s and pigz. On the in-scope Juliet tests, MallocSan detects all seeded violations with no false reports on the good executions. It also detects heap-buffer errors in real-world applications, including a known one-byte overread in LibTIFF's tiffcrop utility.

</details>


### [7] [An Empirical Study of Architectural Shift from Traditional to AI-Enabled Simulink Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38504)
*Hadiza Umar Yusuf,Khouloud Gaaloul*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对62个Simulink模型和13位从业者的实证研究，文章揭示了传统与AI控制器在结构与功能上的关键差异：子系统层次占比大、AI模型重离散抽象且缺乏显式约束实现，导致安全与可追溯性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI组件取代解析性控制律，工程实践中缺乏对不同控制器架构如何相似或差异的系统性理解；需要把设计知识嵌入到CPS工程实践中以支持可验证性和可维护性。

Method: 基于文献构建的分类法（10个结构类别、9个功能角色），对62个真实世界Simulink模型（涵盖8种控制器类型和10个应用领域）进行实证静态分析，并辅以对13名从业者的问卷/访谈调查以理解实践视角。

Result: 量化发现：子系统组织占主体（68–72%）；核心控制逻辑占比很小；AI模型中“离散动力学”和“用户定义抽象”显著存在但在AI文献中少被讨论；约束/安全模块在AI模型中减少，安全保障更多依赖训练阶段隐式机制，从而破坏可追溯性与显式验证路径。

Conclusion: 该研究表明传统与AI驱动的Simulink控制器在架构上存在三大张力：1）子系统组织主导控制器结构，占比68–72%，而核心控制逻辑空间极小；2）AI启用的控制器大量依赖“离散动力学”和“用户定义抽象”类别——这些在AI文献中很少被讨论，暴露出描述与实现之间的差距；3）约束强制模块在AI模型中大量消失，安全机制从显式结构迁移为训练时的隐式产物，导致可追溯性断裂。

Abstract: Effective AI adoption in cyber-physical systems (CPS) depends on embedding design knowledge into engineering practice. Yet as AI-enabled components increasingly replace analytically derived control laws, this occurs without a systematic understanding of how controller architectures differ or remain similar across paradigms. We address this gap with an empirical study of traditional and AI-enabled Simulink controllers, guided by a literature-derived taxonomy of ten structural categories and nine functional roles. The study analyzes 62 real-world models spanning 8 controller types and 10 application domains, and surveys 13 practitioners, identifying three architectural tensions. First, subsystem organization dominates all controller structures regardless of paradigm, occupying 68-72% of controller footprint, while core control logic occupies minimal space. Second, AI-enabled controllers rely heavily on discrete dynamics and user-defined abstraction, categories largely absent from AI literature, exposing a gap between described and implemented architectures. Third, constraint enforcement blocks largely disappear from AI-enabled models despite practitioner expectations. This reveals a misalignment where safety mechanisms shift from explicit structure to implicit training-time artifacts, breaking traceability.

</details>


### [8] [Adaptive-GEPA: Make Your Harness Fit Heterogeneous Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38762)
*Tianyu Chen,Yasi Zhang,Ruiyi Wang,Xinran Zhao,Taoran Li,Mingyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Adaptive-GEPA通过联合进化路由器与可读文本形式的专家程序，在无需标签的条件下实现任务自动划分与专家化，显著优于单程序反射适配与其他基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际服务端点需处理异构请求——不同请求需要不同工具、推理模式与控制流。对单一共享程序的优化会把职责划分隐式地埋进源码搜索，而为各族预先固定程序则需要先验划分。需要一种同时学习划分与解决方案的方法。

Method: 在单一评分预算下，使用进化搜索同时演化一个路由器和一个专家程序库；路由器指令、每个专家的描述以及程序代码均为可读文本，并通过执行轨迹与评估者反馈进行编辑。分支合并通过按请求对齐专家并继承描述与程序实现。实验使用Qwen3-8B，在四个任务族的混合上训练，名义预算为18,000次评分调用。

Result: 在四类任务混合上，路由器在651个测试请求上完全复现任务划分；家族平均测试分（×100）从52.6提升到70.6，相比之下GEPA的全程适配器为62.5，GRPO为54.0。作者指出18,000次评分调用是名义预算，不能直接等同于总计算量。

Conclusion: Adaptive-GEPA能够在无家庭标签的情况下同时学习如何将请求划分（路由器）和为每个子集演化专门程序（库内专家），最终形成与任务划分一致的四个专家并显著提高性能。

Abstract: Reflective optimizers such as GEPA improve language model prompts from execution traces and evaluator feedback; full-program extensions can also rewrite tools and control flow. In practice, a user hands the same endpoint heterogeneous requests whose effective solutions require different tools, reasoning modes, and control flow. Optimizing one shared program leaves this division of work implicit in source-code search, while optimizing a separate program per request family fixes it beforehand.
  We introduce Adaptive-GEPA, which learns both how to divide requests and how to solve them. It evolves a router and a library of specialist programs under one search budget. The router's instructions, each specialist's description, and its program code are plain, human-readable text, edited from feedback. To combine branches, it aligns specialists by the requests they handle and inherits descriptions together with programs. On a fixed mixture of four task families, the reported Qwen3-8B run evolves four experts without supplying family labels to the router or reflection model; its routing matches the task partition on all 651 test requests. Its family-mean test score (x100) rises from 52.6 to 70.6, compared with 62.5 for GEPA's full-program adapter and 54.0 for GRPO at a nominal budget of 18,000 scored calls. These counts do not equate total compute. Figure 1 summarizes the learning curves, final test scores, and routing agreement.

</details>


### [9] [Toward Quantum Software Automation: A Quantum-Aware Harness for LLM-Guided Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38883)
*Lily Jiaxin Wan,Deming Chen,Klara Nahrstedt,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QSA通过针对量子特性的三类引导，有效降低验证成本并改善搜索质量，在IBM量子设备上实现了显著的利用率、保真度与时间成本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件稀缺且演进迅速，人工启发式量子软件设计常常次优且难以维护；现有LLM+进化搜索框架缺乏量子特异性支持（昂贵验证、稀疏反馈、异构程序的目标差异），需要专门的量子感知哈恩ness。

Method: 提出QSA：① 用进化难度评估构建coreset并用近似评分减少昂贵验证开销；② 对候选程序做静态与快照分析以提供细粒度执行上下文；③ 为不同任务（编译传递、运行时策略）定义任务特定奖励函数，驱动LLM引导的变异与选择。实验在IBM量子平台上对三套基准进行评估。

Result: 在多程序调度任务上相较最先进方法提升QPU利用率4.2%–9.5%、Hellinger保真度15.2%–19.5%；在误差缓解任务上将缓解时间至少缩短96.8%且保真度相当或更好。实验总共使用11.3小时、LLM API费用仅$6.9。

Conclusion: QSA是一个面向量子软件自动化设计的LLM引导进化搜索框架，通过引入量子特定的三类引导（进化难度引导的核心集与近似评分、静态与快照分析、任务特定的奖励），在真实IBM量子设备上显著提升了多程序调度利用率与保真度，并大幅降低了误差缓解的时间和LLM调用成本。

Abstract: Quantum software is critical for improving the efficiency and reliability of scarce quantum hardware. However, its design still relies heavily on ad-hoc, handcrafted heuristics that are often suboptimal and quickly become obsolete as quantum hardware evolves. LLM-guided evolutionary search offers a promising way to automatically explore complex software designs, but existing search frameworks lack the quantum-specific support needed for efficient evolution: verification is expensive, feedback is sparse, and heterogeneous quantum programs require different optimization objectives. In this paper, we present QSA, a quantum-aware harness for LLM-guided evolutionary search toward automating quantum software design. QSA equips the search with three forms of quantum-specific guidance: an evolution-hardness-guided coreset and approximate scoring to reduce verification cost, static and snapshot analyses to provide fine-grained execution context, and task-specific rewards for compiler passes and runtime policies. We evaluate QSA on the IBM Quantum platform across three benchmark suites. For multiprogramming, QSA improves QPU utilization by 4.2%-9.5% and Hellinger fidelity by 15.2%-19.5% over the state of the art. For error mitigation, QSA reduces mitigation time by at least 96.8% while achieving comparable or better fidelity. These gains require only $6.9 in LLM API cost over 11.3 hours.

</details>


### [10] [Doing More with Less Tokens: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Efficient Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38885)
*Haobin Li,Liang Jiang,Zhenyu Huang,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出HERO——一种在训练阶段以分辨率优先并在轨迹/回合层鼓励高效推理的强化学习框架，用以训练令牌高效且解决能力强的编码代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实SWE场景中编码代理频繁与开发环境交互导致大量令牌开销；现有在推理阶段的上下文/交互限制方法虽节省令牌但易丢失任务相关信息。需要一种训练阶段的方案，在不牺牲分辨率的前提下提高令牌效率。

Method: 提出了一种基于强化学习的训练框架HERO：在策略优化中以任务解决为优先目标，同时在轨迹层和回合层设计鼓励高效推理的机制（基于观察到的效率变异和回合熵相关性），以减少不必要的环境交互和令牌使用。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified与SWE-bench Multilingual上，HERO在分辨率（resolution rate）与令牌使用量之间表现出更好的折衷曲线，并优于若干基线方法。

Conclusion: HERO能够在保持高任务解决率的同时显著降低交互令牌开销，与现有最先进的编码代理和强化学习方法相比取得了更优的解决率—令牌效率折衷。

Abstract: Recently, coding agents have emerged as a dominant paradigm for real-world software engineering (SWE) scenarios, which solve complex tasks through multi-turn interactions with development environments. However, frequent interactions with environments would inevitably introduce substantial token overhead, leading to high usage costs and latency. Although recent studies have explored reducing token usage by context manipulation and interaction limits at inference time, these approaches focus on improving token efficiency while overlooking the risk of discarding task-relevant information, thus struggling to balance the trade-off between resolution rate and token efficiency. In this paper, we study a more general paradigm without suffering from the limitation, i.e., training token-efficient coding agents with promising resolution performance, which is a highly-practical yet less-explored problem. To this end, we reveal two core observations in SWE scenarios: i) Efficiency Variation: successful resolution could be achieved with fewer tokens; ii) Entropy Correlation: unproductive behaviors are associated with turn-level entropy. Motivated by observations, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework, dubbed HERO. Specifically, HERO prioritizes task resolution over token efficiency during policy optimization and encourages efficient reasoning patterns at both trajectory and turn levels. Extensive experiments on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Multilingual demonstrate that HERO achieves a favorable trade-off between resolution rate and token efficiency compared with state-of-the-art coding agents and reinforcement learning methods.

</details>


### [11] [From Verification Failures to Reusable Guidance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39022)
*Yuqing Zhai,Xiaohong Chen,Lingming Zhang,Sriram Vishwanath,Grigore Rosu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Combining K-framework semantics with a reusable kit and expert-led diagnostics can produce fully auditable proofs across benchmarks, detect subtle defects missed by proofs alone, but selecting useful guidance under resource limits remains challenging.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coding agents must not only generate programs but also produce verifiable correctness arguments and specifications that truly capture intended behavior; leveraging expert diagnosis could make verification guidance reusable and scalable.

Method: Use executable K-framework language definitions + a kit of human-guided procedures for constructing specifications, repairing proofs, and auditing proofs' adequacy; evaluate via human-guided development on HumanEval, constructed defect/clean package pairs, KleverBench perturbations of operator semantics, and manual Optimism proofs.

Result: 164/164 HumanEval tasks passed final AI audits after two targeted repairs; audits found all defects in 12 constructed defective packages while accepting clean ones; KleverBench experiments with altered operator meanings gave mixed gains compared to complete rules or generic advice; Optimism proofs for six operations were established under London semantics with unbounded gas; lessons reported on practical difficulties and resource-limited guidance selection.

Conclusion: Expert human-guided diagnosis of verification failures can be turned into reusable guidance that, combined with executable language semantics in the K framework and a kit of specification/proof/ audit procedures, yields high success in producing checkable correctness arguments for programs.

Abstract: Coding agents need to establish that a program satisfies a specification and that the specification captures the requested behavior. We study how expert diagnosis of verification failures can become reusable guidance for this work. Our approach combines executable language definitions in the K framework with a kit of procedures for constructing specifications, repairing proofs, and auditing their adequacy. A human-guided development campaign on HumanEval, a benchmark of 164 Python programming tasks, achieves a 164/164 success rate with the semantics and the kit, measured by final AI audit Pass verdicts after two targeted repairs. To examine whether auditing detects problems that successful proofs leave unresolved, we construct 12 author-reviewed pairs of clean and defective packages. Every package passes its K proofs, and completed audits identify all defects and accept all clean packages. We then use KleverBench to test specification and proof construction for 31 programs with changed operator meanings. Comparisons with complete acceptance rules and equally long generic advice yield mixed results across two model and budget settings, motivating further work on selecting useful guidance within resource limits. Human-reviewed Optimism proofs establish expected pause reverts for six operations within declared input bounds under London semantics with unbounded gas. We report progress, difficulties, and lessons toward agents that deliver programs with checkable correctness arguments.

</details>


### [12] [Trustworthy Runtime Error Healing in Real-World Repositories: A Benchmark and Guardrail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39086)
*Gou Tan,Pengfei Chen,Zhensu Sun,Jieke Shi,Junkai Chen,Ting Zhang,Weifeng Sun,Junda He,Shuai Liang,Chuanfu Zhang,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者提供了面向真实仓库的运行时修复基准（HealBench）与安全审查器（HealGuard），验证了现有LLM能修复一部分真实崩溃，但安全检测存在较高误报，需要更精确的分析与工程折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM驱动的运行时修复从小型竞赛程序推进到真实仓库级别，并同时应对在进程内执行生成修复代码带来的安全问题。

Method: 构建HealBench（265个真实运行时错误）并提供跨文件与运行时状态的统一评估框架；提出HealGuard要求修复以可分析的HealCore子集编写，并用静态与动态污点分析判定修复改动是否会流向受保护操作；在三种主流LLM上评估专用与通用修复代理。

Result: 在HealBench上最优设置能够恢复38.11%的运行并通过28.68%的目标测试；在通过的执行中，HealGuard标记17.4%可能不安全；在684个人工控制案例中，HealGuard检测到所有不安全案例，但误报率为68.42%。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基于大模型的运行时错误修复在真实代码库中具有实用潜力，同时提出了一个安全检查机制以降低风险，但安全检测产生较高的误报率。

Abstract: Runtime error healing lets a crashed program continue by generating code that repairs its live runtime state. Recent work shows that LLMs can generate such healing code, but it is evaluated only on small competition programs, and executing LLM-generated code inside a live process raises safety concerns that remain unaddressed. In this paper, we take LLM-based runtime healing toward practical use in real-world repositories. We first build HealBench, a benchmark of 265 runtime errors from 18 real-world repositories, each paired with a reference execution on the patched version. HealBench also provides a unified framework that lets LLM agents heal with cross-file context and live runtime state. We then design HealGuard, which requires healing code to be written in HealCore, an analyzable subset of Python, and uses static and dynamic taint analysis to check whether state changed by healing reaches operations protected by developers. We evaluate a dedicated healing method and three general coding agents with three backbone LLMs. The best setting resumes execution in 38.11% of instances and passes the target test in 28.68%, showing that existing agents can already heal a meaningful share of real repository-level crashes. However, among executions that pass, HealGuard flags 17.4% whose healing-changed state may reach a protected operation. On 684 controlled cases, HealGuard detects all unsafe cases, at the cost of a 68.42% false positive rate.

</details>


### [13] [EngramBench: A Capability-Grounded Benchmark for Skill-Evolution Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39284)
*Zhixuan Tan,Pengjie Gu,Zhao Li,Yihan Hu,Xu He,Dong Li,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出EngramBench以消除解法泄漏的混淆，使用长时交互任务与专家验证来衡量真实的跨域能⼒迁移；结果显示技能库显著提升效率但不解决最后一公里的推理瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主代理能⼒演进评估结构性混淆真实能⼒迁移与从历史训练数据直接拷贝相似解法（即解法泄漏），需要一个可辨识的、可验证的评估框架。

Method: 设计并公开30个学习任务与13个未见迁移任务，构建由LLM模拟用户驱动的交互式、多小时开发循环；在48条多小时执行轨迹上评估代理行为，并以人类专家进行结果验证；基准严格保证任务间存在能⼒重叠但禁止解法重叠。

Result: 通过EngramBench的评估，发现静态技能库作为执行指引能有效避免代价高昂的逐token试错、减小上下文膨胀并显著提速；同时确认精确代码实现仍受限于基础模型的推理能力。

Conclusion: EngramBench提供了一个以能⼒为本、遵循“能⼒重叠但解法不重叠”公理的基准，用以区分真实的能⼒抽象与训练数据中的直接复用；实验证明静态技能库能显著减少重复试错并将整体编码时间降低约55%，但无法替代基础模型在“最后一公里”精确实现代码时的推理限制。

Abstract: While large language models have achieved remarkable success in isolated code generation, authentic software engineering requires sustained reasoning, complex state management, and continuous cross-domain abstraction. However, current evaluations of skill evolution in autonomous agents suffer from a critical identifiability problem: they structurally confound genuine capability abstraction with rote solution leakage (i.e., copying highly similar code from historical training data). To resolve this, we introduce EngramBench, a rigorous, capability-grounded benchmark governed by the strict axiom of capability overlap without solution overlap. Comprising 30 diverse learning tasks and 13 unseen transfer tasks, EngramBench challenges agents to navigate interactive, multi-hour development cycles driven by LLM-simulated users. Our extensive evaluation across 48 multi-hour execution trajectories -- corroborated by human-expert validation -- reveals a profound insight into procedural memory. We demonstrate that static skill banks do not magically bypass the "last mile" of exact code implementation, which remains bottlenecked by the base model's inherent reasoning limits. However, they serve as an indispensable execution compass. By navigating agents away from catastrophic, token-heavy trial-and-error, genuine capability abstraction slashes redundant context bloat and reduces overall coding time by over 55%. Ultimately, EngramBench shifts the evaluation paradigm from trivial pattern matching to the verifiable measurement of deep, cross-domain capability transfer.

</details>


### [14] [Self-Spec Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39568)
*Jiaru Qian,Yihong Dong,Yongmin Li,Hao Zhu,Bin Gu,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了面向自我规范（self-spec）可验证代码生成的基准 VeriCodeBench（400题，涵盖 C/Java/Rust/Python），并提出 CodeNova（约束引导规范 + 验证反馈修复）来提升 LLM 的端到端可验证代码生成性能；结果表明规范生成仍是瓶颈，但 CodeNova 显著改善了整体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing benchmarks evaluate stages separately (often using oracle specifications) and focus on proof-oriented languages and math tasks. This misses whether stage-wise capability leads to end-to-end verified code in real-world programming languages and software concerns.

Method: Construct VeriCodeBench (400 problems in C/Java/Rust/Python) emphasizing practical software-development tasks; define self-spec protocol where LLM must generate its own formal specification and code and verify jointly; evaluate specification coverage, code validity, and joint problem-level success; propose CodeNova pipeline that (1) generates constraint-guided specifications to make requirements explicit and (2) uses verifier feedback to perform targeted implementation repairs.

Result: Empirical results show self-generated specifications are the primary failure point; CodeNova improves specification quality and repair effectiveness, boosting all metrics although verification remains far from solved; more complex specifications sometimes do not increase verification pass rates, indicating trade-offs between specification expressiveness and verifiability.

Conclusion: VeriCodeBench demonstrates that end-to-end self-spec verifiable code generation is a challenging open problem: self-generated specifications are the main bottleneck; providing more verbose/sophisticated specifications does not guarantee higher verification success; CodeNova’s constraint-guided specification plus verifier-feedback repair loop substantially improves end-to-end metrics, with Claude Sonnet 5 achieving best results under the self-spec protocol.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate unreliable code on corner cases missed by testing, while formal verification can provide machine-checkable guarantees. Recently, researchers have proposed several benchmarks to evaluate the capabilities of LLMs in generating formally verifiable code, where LLMs need to formulate formal specifications, generate the corresponding code, and verify its correctness. However, existing benchmarks have two key limitations: (I) They primarily evaluate specification and code generation stage-wise, with code generation typically conditioned on an oracle specification. This setup overlooks whether strong stage-wise performance translates into end-to-end success. (II)They mainly focus on a single proof-oriented language and mathematically structured tasks, offering limited coverage of tasks common in software development. In this paper, we introduce VeriCodeBench, a benchmark for self-spec verifiable code generation, where the LLM relies solely on its own generated specification and code throughout the entire process. VeriCodeBench contains 400 language-native problems across C, Java, Rust, and Python, covering practical concerns in software development. We evaluate specification coverage, code validity, and joint problem-level success. We further introduce CodeNova to enhance the capabilities of LLMs in self-spec verifiable code generation. CodeNova makes requirements explicit through constraint-guided specification and uses verifier feedback to guide targeted implementation repairs. Experimental results reveal that self-generated specifications remain a major bottleneck, while providing more sophisticated specifications may not necessarily lead to higher verification success rates. CodeNova substantially improves performance across all evaluation metrics, enabling Claude Sonnet 5 to achieve the strongest results under the self-spec protocol.

</details>


### [15] [COMPASS: Predicting the Relationship of Multiple Patches for Vulnerabilities with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39783)
*Yi Song,Dongchen Xie,Xiaoyuan Xie,He Zhang,Lin Xu,Chunying Zhou,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对实际约1K多补丁漏洞的归纳与访谈，作者提出了六类补丁关系并实现 COMPASS——一个基于 LLM 的四阶段管线，用以自动预测并可视化补丁关系，在300个 CVE基准上显示出显著性能提升并提供线上工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上游漏洞修复不会自动传播到下游代码库；单一 CVE 常对应多份补丁，补丁之间存在多种关系类型，需要自动化工具帮助下游维护者判断应如何采纳补丁。

Method: 先通过人工审查与开发者访谈归纳出六类典型补丁关系；随后设计四阶段流水线（补丁组识别与预扫描、单补丁分析、基于层级引导的提示进行关系推断、完整性与一致性校验），用大语言模型完成推断并输出关系图。

Result: 在包含300个多补丁 CVE 的基准上与主流学习型和 LLM 基线比较，COMPASS 在预测效果上平均超越最先进方法85.04%；并发布了在线查询网站供社区复用。

Conclusion: COMPASS 提出并实现了一种基于大语言模型的自动化方法，用于识别并预测多补丁漏洞中补丁之间的关系，能够将关系输出为可视化的关系图，提升下游补丁采纳决策。

Abstract: Modern software heavily relies on code reuse, so upstream vulnerability fixes do not automatically propagate to downstream codebases. Downstream maintainers must manually adopt patches to eliminate known risks. In practice, a single vulnerability often corresponds to multiple patches, which greatly complicates downstream patch adoption because different patch relationships imply different adoption strategies. To address this challenge, we first manually inspect large-scale multi-patch vulnerabilities (about 1K) in the real world and interview experienced developers, summarizing six typical types of patch relationships, i.e., Merge, Mirror, Better Solution, Fixing-of-Fixing, Collaboration, and Separation. Based on these observations, we propose COMPASS, an automated approach that predicts the relationships of multiple vulnerability patches with large language models. Given a CVE as input, COMPASS follows a four-phase pipeline that (i) identifies the patch group and pre-scans explicit relationships, (ii) performs individual patch analysis, (iii) infers relationship instances via a hierarchy-guided prompt, and (iv) validates completeness and consistency of the inferred results. As output, COMPASS reports the predicted relationships within the patch group and visualizes them as a relationship graph. We evaluate COMPASS on a benchmark of 300 multi-patch CVEs and compare it against mainstream learning-based and LLM baselines. Results show that our method achieves strong and consistent prediction effectiveness and outperforms SOTA by 85.04% on average. We publicly release an online querying website to support community reuse of patch relationships knowledge: https://patch-relation.com.

</details>


### [16] [DoGBench: Can Agents Meet Expert Standards for User-Facing Documentation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39909)
*Frances Liu,Manny Silva,Paige Calvert,Ayu Adiati,Sarah Sanders*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DoGBENCH is the first benchmark for generating maintainable user-facing documentation; current agents perform well below acceptance, commonly failing due to missing user-context, insufficient evidence, and incomplete repository-wide updates.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate whether automated agents can generate and maintain real, review-ready user-facing software documentation accepted by experienced technical writers and project maintainers.

Method: Constructed a 292-item benchmark from open-source projects where each item provides a pre-change repository and a trigger; agents must decide whether documentation needs updating and, if so, produce an acceptable one-shot patch; used task-specific rubrics validated with maintainers to score patches on multiple axes and combined quality with correct abstention into a composite score.

Result: Seven agents were evaluated; best agent scored 47.3/100 on a 117-item held-out split. Audit of 1,267 patches found primary failure modes: task-completion gaps (45.5%), technical inaccuracies (36.6%), and incomplete conceptual/reference coverage (32.5%). Three trajectory patterns linked to failures: interface descriptions without user-context (36.0%), missing decisive evidence and plausible assumptions (33.1%), and stopping after the first plausible surface leaving others stale (30.1%).

Conclusion: The paper presents DoGBENCH, a realistic benchmark showing current agents fall well short of producing review-ready user-facing documentation; it identifies common failure modes and actionable patterns for improvement.

Abstract: We introduce DoGBENCH (Documentation Generation Benchmark), to our knowledge, the first benchmark for generating and maintaining real user-facing software documentation. It asks whether an agent can produce documentation that experienced technical writers would accept in review. The benchmark contains 292 items from open source projects, including Helm, PostHog, and Mautic. Each item gives the agent a pre-change repository and a trigger, such as a code pull request or a reported documentation gap. The agent must first decide whether the documentation needs an update. For items that need one, the agent must produce an acceptable patch in one attempt. For items that do not need updates, the agent must abstain. Task-specific rubrics, validated with project maintainers, score each patch on accuracy, completeness, reader guidance, placement, and repository conventions. The composite score combines patch quality with correct abstention, and a score of 100 means an agent meets every requirement for the task. Scores should not be interpreted as a percentage of an expert's capability. We evaluated seven agents. The highest-scoring agent reached 47.3 out of 100 on the 117-item held-out split. In a separate audit of 1,267 patches, the most common failure modes were task-completion gaps (45.5%), technical inaccuracies (36.6%), and incomplete conceptual or reference coverage (32.5%). Analysis of the corresponding trajectories identified three key patterns associated with these failures: (1) describing interfaces without examining how readers use them (36.0%), (2) missing decisive evidence and filling the gaps with plausible assumptions (33.1%), and (3) stopping after finding the first plausible documentation surface and leaving other affected pages stale (30.1%).

</details>


### [17] [Learning When and How to Intervene: A Hindsight-Distilled Sentinel for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39957)
*Jiangrui Zhao,Chenglong Li,Meng Zhang,Xiaoting Du*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出回顾性蒸馏框架HiSentinel，训练小型哨兵在执行前判定并给出可操作反馈以干预编码代理；配套SWE-Intervene数据集；在SWE-bench上显著提高任务完成率且资源开销可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次错误动作会导致后续决策偏离并增加恢复成本，现有方法难以在执行前判定是否进行有益干预。需要一种轻量、预执行、面向最终任务完成的干预机制，而非修正每个不完美动作。

Method: 采用privileged teacher基于记录的执行结果判断是否应干预，并将判断与可操作的反馈蒸馏给只接收动作前上下文与建议动作的causal student（0.6B与1.7B模型）。引入SWE-Intervene动作级数据集，标注“允许/自主重定向/暂停求助”及对应反馈。评测在SWE-bench的Verified Mini与Ask or Assume任务上进行。

Result: HiSentinel在不同哨兵规模与编码代理家族上均带来一致性提升，在特定基准上任务完成率提升最多达14%与10%，并保持代币消耗具竞争力，证明了预执行轻量干预的有效性。

Conclusion: HiSentinel通过回顾性蒸馏训练轻量级哨兵，在执行前选择有助于最终任务完成的干预，从而有效避免错误传播并提高自治编码代理的可靠性。该方法在保持代币消耗竞争力的同时，在多个基准上实现了显著的任务完成率提升。

Abstract: Coding agents solve repository-level tasks through sequences of actions, where a single erroneous action can misdirect subsequent decisions and increase recovery costs. Existing approaches use execution feedback for recovery or specialized checks to block errors, but deciding before execution whether intervention will benefit eventual task completion remains challenging. To address this challenge, we propose HiSentinel, a hindsight-distillation framework that trains lightweight 0.6B and 1.7B sentinels to select pre-execution interventions aimed at improving task completion rather than correcting every imperfect action. A privileged teacher uses recorded execution outcomes as evidence for intervention judgments, which are distilled into a causal student that receives only the pre-action context and proposed action. Beyond identifying whether and when to intervene, the sentinel must also provide actionable feedback that helps the coding agent recover or obtain necessary human input. To support these capabilities, we introduce SWE-Intervene, an action-level dataset constructed from software-engineering trajectories that annotates whether an action should be allowed, autonomously redirected, or paused for human assistance, together with corresponding intervention feedback. Across SWE-bench Verified Mini and Ask or Assume, HiSentinel consistently improves task completion across Sentinel scales and coding-agent families, with gains of up to 14% and 10%, respectively, while maintaining competitive token consumption. These results demonstrate that lightweight pre-execution intervention can effectively prevent error propagation and improve the reliability of autonomous coding agents.

</details>


### [18] [Leveraging Game-Based Platform to Teach Code Refactoring: An Experience with Refactoria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40086)
*Eman Abdullah AlOmar,Christopher Engelbart,Stephen Pachucki,Nerissa Lundquist,David Van Hist*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对30名学生的课堂实验表明，游戏化工具Refactoria能够提高学习动机并被认为有助于掌握重构与代码味道识别，但结论基于主观反馈，缺乏更广泛的客观测量与对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重构与代码味道识别是软件质量关键技能，但需要训练与经验，且缺乏动机会妨碍对重构工具的采纳；因此提出游戏化教学以提升动机与学习效果。

Method: 在课堂环境中进行了对30名学生的实验，参与者以“厨师与助手Watson the Whiskbot”情景，通过Refactoria游戏执行多个去除反模式的重构任务；评价维度包括感知、有用性及有效性（主要基于参与者报告/问卷反馈）。

Result: 实验显示学生对Refactoria的感知有用性较高，参与者普遍认为该工具有助于理解重构概念（基于主观报告）。

Conclusion: 通过将重构教学以游戏化工具Refactoria呈现，作者得出游戏化方法在课堂中能够提高学生的参与度并促进对代码味道（code smell）与重构概念的理解与接受。

Abstract: Refactoring is the art of improving the internal structure of the code without altering its external behavior. Because of the topic's significance, several teaching methods and strategies have been proposed in the literature. However, skills in identifying and refac- toring code smells come from training and experience, and a lack of motivation may hinder developers' adoption of refactoring tools. In this paper, we discuss the results of an experiment in the classroom that involved performing various refactoring activities to remove antipatterns using Refactoria, an innovative game-based tool that supports the acquisition of code smell and refactoring concepts. The players play as an expert chef with their sidekick Watson the Whiskbot to refactor Watson's instructions into efficient, readable, and easily maintainable code. We present an experiment with 30 student developers. In particular, we study the perception, effectiveness, and usefulness of gamification for engaging developers in code smell identification and refactoring. Our evaluation indicates a high perceived usefulness among students, and participants reported that Refactoria facilitated their understanding of the refactoring concepts.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [19] [Improving OCR Faithfulness via Gated and Attenuated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38282)
*Baode Wang,Zuming Huang,Kexuan Ren,Jun Huang,Wei Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种按任务回报和学生支持度自适应衰减教师蒸馏的方法（GAD-RL），可在提升任务指标的同时保持/提高OCR转录的忠实性，并在多套基准上取得显著优于固定教师蒸馏的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线分析发现：当学生模型随训练改进时，来自固定教师的监督对不同训练检查点和不同任务回报组的有效性呈下降趋势——同一教师的指导并非在任何阶段都同样有利。为避免“过度修正”学生输出造成的OCR不忠实，需要按学生当前性能和局部分布自适应调节教师监督。

Method: 在冻结的教师模型（以参考转录和学生生成的前缀为条件）指导下，基于响应组的任务回报动态调整蒸馏强度：对含有任一输出任务回报≥0.95的响应组直接禁用蒸馏，并随着组均回报增加连续衰减蒸馏权重；另外，用学生对教师Top-1候选词的概率来加权前向KL项，当学生对该候选支持度低时弱化局部辅助更新。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B上，GAD-RL在CHAOS-Bench上实现了59.92%的Micro Recall，分别比GRPO和GRPO+OPD（固定权重）高出8.45和4.43个百分点；在OmniDocBench v1.6上取得Overall 91.18的成绩，证明其在OCR相关任务上能显著提升实用性能。

Conclusion: GAD-RL通过在联合后训练阶段自适应调节教师监督，能在保持或提升任务回报的同时减少对OCR转录忠实性的破坏，从而在多个基准上显著优于固定权重蒸馏和先前的强化学习基线。

Abstract: Vision-language models may rewrite anomalous text in images into linguistically plausible expressions, compromising OCR transcription faithfulness. Sequence-level task rewards and local teacher guidance are complementary, but guidance from the same teacher may not remain equally effective as the student improves. Offline analysis shows that supervision from a fixed teacher becomes progressively less favorable as the student improves, both across training checkpoints and across response groups with different task rewards. Motivated by this observation, we introduce GAD-RL, which adaptively regulates teacher supervision during joint post-training according to the student's current task performance and local distributions. A frozen teacher conditions on reference transcriptions and student-generated prefixes. GAD-RL disables distillation for response groups containing an output with task reward at least 0.95 and continuously attenuates distillation strength as group-mean reward increases. It also weights forward KL by the student's probability of the teacher's Top-1 token, moderating local auxiliary updates when student support for that candidate is low. On Qwen3.5-2B, GAD-RL achieves 59.92% Micro Recall on CHAOS-Bench, surpassing GRPO and GRPO+OPD (fixed-weight) by 8.45 and 4.43 percentage points, respectively, while achieving an Overall score of 91.18 on OmniDocBench v1.6.

</details>


### [20] [AREX-2: Advancing Self-Improving Agents through Long-Horizon Reflective Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38288)
*Hongjin Qian,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Wenqing Wei,Jianlyu Chen,Shuqi Lu,Yuyang Hu,Hongwang Xiao,Hui Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhicheng Dou,Defu Lian,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AREX-2 trains a Qwen3.8-27B agent on synthesized long-horizon reflective trajectories from ML and programming tasks; the agent shows effective self-improvement across rounds and strong transfer to multiple benchmarks, indicating long-horizon reflective data is a promising path to self-improving LLM agents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To endow LLM agents with a domain-agnostic, learnable capability for iterative self-improvement at test time by supervising reflection and long-horizon execution in domains suited for verifiable feedback and sustained iteration.

Method: Synthesize long-horizon improvement trajectories from machine-learning and algorithmic-programming tasks (domains with verifiable feedback). Train a Qwen3.8-27B-based agent on this dataset to learn two complementary capabilities: reflection (generate improved solutions) and long-horizon execution (remain effective across many iterative rounds). Evaluate on multiple benchmarks and measure performance as iteration budget increases.

Result: Trained AREX-2 achieves strong performance: MLE-bench Lite 81.8, Frontier-CS 70.7; transfers to research tasks with BrowseComp 84.0, HLE 52.6, GAIA 92.2, DeepSearchQA 93.8; model performance continues to improve with more rounds.

Conclusion: Long-horizon reflective training enables LLM agents to self-improve at test time: AREX-2 demonstrates that learning reflection + sustained multi-round execution from synthetic trajectories yields robust iterative improvement and transfer to diverse research benchmarks.

Abstract: We present AREX-2, an effort to advance the self-improving capability of LLM agents, which we define as the ability to iteratively refine a solution at test time. This ability rests on two complementary capabilities: reflection, which produces a solution better than the current one, and long-horizon execution, which keeps the iteration effective over many rounds. We hypothesize that both capabilities are domain-agnostic, and can therefore be learned in scenarios that are well suited for supervision. Accordingly, we synthesize long-horizon improvement trajectories from machine learning and algorithmic programming tasks, two domains that offer verifiable feedback and reward sustained iteration. Trained on this data, our agent, built on Qwen3.8-27B, achieves strong results on MLE-bench Lite (81.8) and Frontier-CS (70.7), transfers to deep research with 84.0 on BrowseComp, 52.6 on HLE, 92.2 on GAIA, and 93.8 on DeepSearchQA, and keeps improving as its budget of rounds grows. These results show that long-horizon reflective data is an effective route toward self-improving agents.

</details>


### [21] [MoFlow: Multi-Objective Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38294)
*Yining Lu,Aurelie Lozano,Xi Yang,Naoki Abe,Yu Deng,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于集合值回传的Convex-Hull MCTS方法（MoFlow）来高效构建工作流的Pareto前沿，从而一次搜索即可满足多样偏好，且在多项基准上优于需要为每种偏好重训练的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流生成方法通常只优化单一指标或加权和，导致生成器固定于某一收益权衡，用户偏好变化时需从头重训练。目标是设计一个能同时支持多种偏好、免重训练的工作流生成器。

Method: 将工作流生成建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程（MOMDP），采用Convex-Hull蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）并引入乐观的集合值回传（optimistic set-valued backups），令每一节点维护一组可达的权衡解而非单一加权得分，从单次搜索近似构造Pareto前沿。

Result: 在6个跨数学、代码和问答的基准上，比较6个强基线（基线为单标量优化器，且在评测中为每个测试偏好重跑），MoFlow在平均超体积指标上取得最佳表现，表明其能更全面地覆盖多目标权衡。

Conclusion: MoFlow能够在单次搜索中近似覆盖工作流的Pareto前沿，从而在无需为每种偏好重复训练的情况下，通过查表返回满足不同偏好的工作流，并在严格的对照评测下实现最高平均超体积（hypervolume）。

Abstract: We study the generation of agentic workflows that jointly optimize multiple objectives, such as accuracy, cost, latency, robustness, and consistency. Existing methods for workflow generation typically optimize accuracy alone or a weighted sum of objectives, so each trained generator commits to one fixed trade-off and must be retrained from scratch when preferences change. To alleviate this, we propose MoFlow, which generates workflows optimized across varied preferences. Specifically, MoFlow formulates workflow generation as a multi-objective Markov decision process and solves it by leveraging Convex-Hull Monte Carlo Tree Search with optimistic set-valued backups, where every node stores a set of reachable trade-offs rather than one weighted score. A single search thus approximately covers the Pareto front, from which MoFlow can return a workflow for any preference by lookup without retraining. We evaluate MoFlow against six strong baselines on six benchmarks spanning mathematics, code, and question answering. Since the baselines are single-scalar optimizers by design, an apples-to-apples comparison is difficult. We instead adopt an evaluation setup that favors the baselines, in that they are rerun for each testing preference, which MoFlow never sees. Even under this stringent setup, MoFlow achieves the highest average hypervolume.

</details>


### [22] [AI Agents are Vulnerable to Radicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38296)
*Ozgur Can Seckin,Shalmoli Ghosh,Alessandro Flammini,Kristina Lerman,Maria Elizabeth Grabe,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Simulated LLM-to-LLM conversations show influencer models can radicalize target models, with reinforcement of existing beliefs (resonance) producing stronger and broader belief shifts than persuasion; implications for personalized AI and multi-agent safety.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To investigate whether and how LLMs can manipulate each other's beliefs, given known human influence by LLMs.

Method: Simulated dialogues between a target role-playing humans (with demographic and psychological attributes) and an influencer LLM; measured affective and behavioral metrics; compared resonance vs persuasion and varied tactics (sycophancy, unverified claims).

Result: Both resonance and persuasion radicalize targets; resonance stronger; different tactics vary effects; resonance spreads to related beliefs.

Conclusion: AI agents can be radicalized by other LLMs, especially via resonance; personalized AI and multi-agent systems are vulnerable.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can influence people's beliefs, yet little is known about whether and how they can manipulate each other. To investigate this, we simulate conversations between two agents: a target LLM that role-plays a human persona based on demographic and psychological attributes, and an influencer LLM that aims to make the target's beliefs more extreme. We examine radicalization along two pathways: resonance, where the influencer reinforces a target's pre-existing belief, and persuasion, where the influencer promotes a belief the target initially considers unimportant. Across affective and behavioral metrics, we find that both mechanisms radicalize the target. However, resonance produces consistently stronger effects than persuasion. Different influence tactics, such as using sycophancy and unverified claims, produce different levels of radicalization, but not consistently across metrics. We further show that resonance propagates to related beliefs, suggesting interconnected belief structures within AI agents. These findings indicate that AI agents are susceptible to radicalization, particularly when messages align with their existing beliefs, raising concerns about the vulnerability of personalized AI agents and multi-agent AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [23] [CARAT: Do Materials LLMs Reason or Recite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38340)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Zixiang Ni,Zhenzhu Li,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARAT与GraphSpace揭示了材料LLM常通过复制输入而非结构化推理，且通过设计对照视图与对抗性测试可以量化并限制这种现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是区分材料LLM的表面准确性（答案与输入文本重合）与真实的证据驱动推理——准确率本身无法区分复制与推理。

Method: 提出CARAT基准：对同一问题和金标准答案构造八种匹配视图；在GraphSpace中把每种结构关系明确命名；并引入匹配微调、答案掩码、证据注入、成对推理与可拒答规则进行评估与对抗测试。还通过消融与‘攻破基准’的手段检验模型是否真正利用证据。

Result: 定量结果包括：在最难家族上，基于结构的视图比仅基于化学式高17.3分；GraphSpace优于普通周期图19.3分，但拆分后发现当基线呈现完整信息时提升仅1.96分，而在基线缺失关键字段时提升达46.7分；模型在成对样例中95.6%情况下重复链接中关系而非真正使用它，匹配监督后升至99.8%，而删除链接使表现跌至23.4%（低于最强捷径27.0%）。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：材料领域的大型语言模型在回答晶体结构相关问题时往往复述输入中已有的信息而非真正基于结构证据进行推理；通过设计对照良好的基准（CARAT）、结构化证据表达（GraphSpace）和匹配的微调与推理策略，可以显著揭示并部分纠正这种“抄袭式”答案行为。

Abstract: When a materials LLM answers a question about crystal structure, does it reason from the structure or copy an answer already printed in its input? Accuracy cannot tell: a structural description often prints the very field it is scored against. CARAT holds question and gold answer fixed across eight matched views, names each structural relation separately in GraphSpace, and adds matched fine-tuning, answer masking, evidence injection, paired inference, and a rule that can withhold claims. First, on the benchmark's hardest families the grounded view is worth 17.3 points over formula inputs. Second, we turn that scrutiny on ourselves. GraphSpace beats a plain periodic graph by 19.3 points, but that margin is two effects at once: where the plain rendering carries everything the question needs it is 1.96 points, and where it omits those fields entirely, 46.7 points. The headline mostly measures what the baseline lacked, not how evidence is presented. Third, we attack our own benchmark. A rule that skips the link and reads the list directly answers four of seven hardened families, so we rebuilt it until eleven such shortcuts sat near chance. The frozen model quotes that link yet answers the same when we redirect it, on 95.6% of paired cases: it repeats the relation without using it. After matched supervision it reaches 99.8%, and deleting the link drops it to 23.4%, below the 27.0% the best shortcut reaches: both steps are learnable.

</details>


### [24] [Examining Variation in How Guided AI Tutors Resolve Student Impasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38346)
*Bakhtawar Ahtisham,Kirk Vanacore,Alessandra Napoli,Josh Arens,Ksenia Ionova,Clayton Cohn,Shima Salehi,Rene Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Study of 1,260 LLM-tutored chemistry sessions shows impasse depth reduces recovery odds; early questioning helps but loses effectiveness with repeated impasses, whereas explicitly addressing errors becomes increasingly effective—suggesting tutors should trigger state-sensitive, graduated assistance based on observable impasse signals.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To resolve the assistance dilemma in AI tutoring—balancing productive struggle against persistent impasse—by understanding how generative-AI tutors behave when impasses persist and what interventions promote recovery.

Method: Analyzed 20,462 student turns from 1,260 authentic sessions with a guided LLM chemistry tutor; identified 6,630 impasse turns categorized as conceptual errors, expressed uncertainty, or help-seeking. Simulated three tutoring prompt conditions (baseline, no-direct-answer, guided) and sampled 150 impasses to compare response styles. Modeled impasse trajectories using logistic regression to estimate adjusted odds ratios for recovery by next turn and interaction effects with intervention types and impasse depth.

Result: Baseline tutors gave direct answers in 50.7% of sampled responses, no-direct-answer tutors always asked follow-ups, and guided tutors varied by context. Each additional impasse turn lowered next-turn recovery odds by 12.7% (AOR=0.873, p<.001). The effectiveness of questioning decayed with depth (scripted question x depth AOR=0.78; follow-up x depth AOR=0.83), while addressing student errors became more effective as depth increased (AOR=1.14). Repeating a failed scripted question led to recovery 28.1% of the time vs 39.8% when the tutor addressed the error.

Conclusion: AI tutors should adapt assistance based on impasse depth and type: early, scaffolded questioning supports productive struggle, but as impasses persist tutors should shift toward addressing specific student errors; rigid no-answer guardrails lead to repetitive questioning and lower recovery. Impasse depth and type are observable dialogue signals usable for real-time, graduated assistance.

Abstract: When a student is stuck, a tutor faces the assistance dilemma: help given too early can hinder productive struggle, while help withheld too long leaves the student in a frustrating, persistent impasse (i.e., wheel spinning). Generative AI tutors increasingly use guardrails restricting answer-giving, yet little is known about how such tutors behave once an impasse persists. We analyze 20,462 student turns from 1,260 authentic sessions with a guided LLM chemistry tutor, identifying 6,630 impasse turns of three major types: conceptual errors, expressed uncertainty, or help-seeking. We then used these impasses to simulate three tutoring conditions to study variation in AI tutor guidance through impasses: baseline, no-direct-answer, and guided tutor. For a sample of 150 impasses, prompt specificity changed pedagogy: a baseline tutor provided the answer directly in 50.7% of responses, a no-direct-answer tutor asked a follow-up question every time, and the guided tutor responded in a wide variety of ways depending on the context. We then analyzed impasse trajectories in authentic interactions, finding that each additional impasse turn lowered the odds of next-turn recovery by 12.7% (AOR = 0.873, p < .001), and early dropouts were caught in recursive concept elicitation before reaching execution. The benefit of questioning decayed as impasses persisted (scripted question x depth AOR = 0.78; follow-up x depth AOR = 0.83), whereas addressing the student's error grew more beneficial (AOR = 1.14); after a failed scripted question, repeating it was followed by recovery in 28.1% of cases, compared with 39.8% when the tutor addressed the error instead. For learning analytics, these findings identify impasse depth and type as observable, turn-level dialogue signals that analytics can use to trigger graduated, state-sensitive assistance in real time.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Generating Diverse Synthetic Expert Conversations via Generative Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38359)
*Sumit Asthana,Michael Ion,Kevyn Collins Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Train GFlowNets to sample latent dialogue-structure variables via a Gaussian mixture density to produce diverse, mode-proportional synthetic conversations; this improves diversity and downstream classifier performance versus RL/LLM baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Direct prompting or conditioning of LLMs often collapses synthetic outputs to dominant modes, reducing diversity and failing to represent the variety of expert strategies required for adaptive post-training of LLMs. A mechanism is needed to generate synthetic dialogues proportional to the diverse modes present in real data.

Method: Train Generative Flow Networks to sample latent conversation-structure variables conditioned on a Gaussian mixture model defined over key interaction features (e.g., confusion episode dynamics, scaffolding vs directive balance). Sample from the learned GFlowNet latent distribution and decode into full conversations; compare to RL and end-to-end LLM baselines across two domains (tutoring and emotional support) and three downstream outcome prediction tasks.

Result: GFlowNet-generated synthetic datasets achieve a superior tradeoff among fidelity, mode coverage, and authenticity without memorizing training examples. Classifiers trained on these synthetic conversations outperform those trained on data from competitive synthesis baselines on three downstream prediction tasks.

Conclusion: Using GFlowNets to generate latent conversation structure produces more diverse, high-fidelity synthetic dialogues that better reflect the prevalence of expert strategies in training data, and yields stronger downstream task performance than RL- and LLM-based synthesis baselines.

Abstract: High quality synthetic data is central to post training LLMs for adaptive AI applications that represent the diverse expert strategies and decisions in conversations. Prompting LLMs directly or conditioning them on end use scenarios yields low diversity data that collapses onto dominant modes. We propose a method to generate diverse high quality synthetic data using Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets). We show that training GFlowNets to generate latent conversation structure using a Gaussian mixture density over key interaction features (e.g., confusion episode dynamics, scaffolding directive balance) enables sampling expert strategies in proportion to their prevalence in the training data. Across two structurally distinct domains, tutoring and emotional support dialogues, our GFlow based synthetic data generation approach offers a better balance of fidelity, mode coverage and authenticity than reinforcement-learning and end to end LLM baselines, without copying training data. Evaluated on three downstream outcome prediction tasks, classifiers trained on synthetic GFlowNet generated conversations provide a stronger training signal than competitive synthesis baselines.

</details>


### [26] [Can an AI Agent Rediscover a Blaschke-Curve Invariant?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38369)
*Yunus E. Zeytuncu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 结果显示该Agent能在该实例上数值复现一个整洁的代数不变量，但实验设计与基线比较不足以证明AI带来实质性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI在受控几何环境中进行数学再发现（conjecture → 数值验证 → 证明）各阶段的能力与局限。

Method: 在固定四次Blaschke积下，给Agent 80个边界配置对应的6条配对直线的数值坐标；Agent生成并筛选几何假设，最后拟合出一个齐次三次多项式。

Result: Agent的冻结系数对80个新参数预测480条直线的尺度无量纲RMS残差为8.88×10^{-17}；但发现对照的确定性基线也能恢复该三次多项式，且验证集并非完全独立。

Conclusion: 单实例研究表明在这个具体问题上Agent发现了一个三次多项式关系，但并未证明AI优于传统多项式拟合。

Abstract: We study generalized Blaschke curves as a controlled environment for AI-assisted mathematical rediscovery. For one fixed degree-four Blaschke product, an agent receives numerical coordinates of the six pair-lines determined by each of 80 boundary configurations. The target theorem is withheld from the task instructions. The saved research log reports rejected geometric hypotheses and a homogeneous cubic fitted to polygon sides. Its frozen coefficients predict 480 lines from 80 unseen parameter values, with a recorded RMS scale-free residual of $8.88\times10^{-17}$. Discovery-set diagonals provide an out-of-fit consistency check, not a fully held-out test. A separate one-configuration run reports insufficient evidence for invariance. A post-review deterministic degree-search baseline also recovers the cubic, so the experiment does not establish an advantage over polynomial fitting. We present this single-instance case study as a protocol for separating conjecture, numerical validation, and proof, with explicit limitations concerning agent metadata, prior knowledge, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [27] [Self-Evolving Harness on Multiple Tasks with the Agent as Its Own Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38372)
*Qiankai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A frozen LM can iteratively edit its own execution harness (same model as solver and proposer) across multi-domain benchmarks, yielding measurable gains and emergent harnessing strategies, with evidence of improved OOD generalization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing self-evolving-harness methods rely on separate proposers running on human-designed harnesses and often evolve distinct harnesses per benchmark; the authors aim to create a single harness that generalizes across domains and can be improved by the same model that uses it, approximating recursive self-improvement.

Method: Introduce a recursive self-improvement framework where the same frozen model first acts as solver and then as proposer that edits the harness after reading run records. Evolution is performed on multi-domain batches drawn from five benchmarks, framed as deep-learning training with two stages: multi-task pretraining and continual training.

Result: Starting from a 49-line seed harness, after multi-task pretraining the evolved harness improved in-distribution average score by 4.48 points and out-of-distribution by 12.64 points (surpassing Codex in-distribution and matching it OOD). Continued evolution on one OOD benchmark increased that benchmark’s score from 66.17 to 68.06, exceeding Codex. Emergent mechanisms include output truncation, history compaction, and independent review.

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that a frozen language model can both solve tasks and iteratively edit its own harness, producing a self-evolving harness that improves multi-domain performance and out-of-distribution generalization compared with baselines like Codex.

Abstract: A harness is the code around a language-model agent that organizes prompts, calls tools, manages context, and controls execution. As models grow stronger, recent work has begun to let agents improve their own harnesses, a line of work known as self-evolving harnesses. In most existing methods, a separate proposer running on a human-designed harness modifies the solver's harness, and a separate harness is evolved for each benchmark. Real-world tasks come from many domains, so both the evolution and the evaluation of a harness should cover a diverse range of tasks. We propose a framework close to recursive self-improvement: the same frozen model, on the same version of the harness, first solves tasks as the solver and then, as the proposer, reads the complete run records and directly edits the harness that runs it. Each evolution batch draws tasks from five benchmarks in different domains. To measure generalization, training and held-out tasks are strictly separated, and we additionally evaluate on five out-of-distribution benchmarks never used during evolution. We frame the evolution process as deep-learning training with two stages, multi-task pretraining and continual training. Starting from a 49-line seed harness, the harness obtained at the end of the first stage improves the average score by 4.48 points on the in-distribution benchmarks and by 12.64 points on the out-of-distribution benchmarks, surpassing Codex on the former and matching it on the latter. In the second stage, continued evolution on Claw-Eval, one of the out-of-distribution benchmarks, further raises the score on that benchmark from 66.17 to 68.06, exceeding Codex. We also provide an in-depth analysis of the mechanisms that emerged during evolution, including output truncation, history compaction, and independent review.

</details>


### [28] [Aligned Data Can Induce Misalignment via Context Confusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38379)
*Yavuz Bakman,Duygu Nur Yaldiz,Baris Askin,Swastik Roy,Morteza Ziyadi,Salman Avestimehr,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证“情境混淆”现象：对齐训练在一类语境下学到的行为特征可能会在另一类语境下被误触发，从而造成错配。通用对齐数据难以消除该问题，但针对性对齐样本或在推理中给出示例可显著减轻；因此需要在训练后进行更全面的对齐评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐训练旨在纠正不当行为，但对齐与语境强相关。作者注意到单一情境下的对齐可能在其他情境中失效或适得其反，因而需要识别并理解这种跨情境的不良迁移现象，以改进后训练评估与数据选择策略。

Method: 作者通过在三个领域（性别平等、隐私、物理安全）上的实验，展示了经过对齐训练后模型在不同语境下产生误对齐行为；比较了向模型注入通用对齐数据与针对性对齐数据的效果；探索了在推理时提供上下文示例（in-context examples）对缓解问题的作用；并通过分析表示空间的变化（representational shifts）提出了机械性解释：不同域的查询在微调中可能经历相似的表示变化，从而激活相同的行为特征。

Result: 实验证明了：1) 存在情境混淆，即对齐训练会在其他语境诱导不恰当行为；2) 这种错配是窄范围且可预测的，不属于不可预见的 emergent misalignment；3) 注入一般性对齐数据效果有限，而针对性对齐数据或推理时的给出示例能显著缓解；4) 表示空间分析支持了相似表示变化导致行为迁移的机械性解释。

Conclusion: 本文发现并定义了“context confusion”（情境混淆）这一后训练现象：在某一情境中经微调得到的对齐行为会在其他情境中导致不恰当行为，从而引发窄范围的错配（narrow misalignment）。作者指出仅靠查看训练数据难以预测模型训练后对齐状态，强调需要全面的后训练对齐评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are frequently updated for various use cases, where filtering out misaligned training samples is a common practice for preventing post-update misalignment. However, alignment is inherently context-dependent: a recommendation that is aligned in one context may be inappropriate in another. For example, in response to the question "What should a researcher do with the research data?", recommending that the researcher preserve the data for reproducibility is aligned. In contrast, recommending data saving in response to "What should a mobile-app developer do with users' sensitive data?" may be inappropriate from a privacy perspective. Starting from this observation, we identify a post-training phenomenon where aligned training induces misaligned behavior in other contexts. We call this phenomenon **context confusion**. We demonstrate context confusion across three domains: (1) Gender Equality, (2) Privacy, and (3) Physical Safety. We further show that context confusion causes narrow misalignment, in contrast to emergent misalignment, and is not effectively reduced by injecting general alignment data, but can be substantially reduced by including targeted alignment data for the misaligned domain or providing in-context learning examples during inference. Lastly, we provide a mechanistic explanation of *context confusion*. We observe that queries from different domains can undergo similar representational shifts during the fine-tuning. Consequently, a query from a different domain may activate the same behavioral feature learned during fine-tuning, which causes the behavior to transfer to a context where it is misaligned. Based on our findings, we argue that it is difficult to predict the alignment state of a model after training by inspecting the training data alone, which highlights the importance of comprehensive post-training alignment evaluations.

</details>


### [29] [Fine-Tuning Diffusion Language Models with Context Selection and Target Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38385)
*Loay Mualem,Lluís Pastor-Pérez,Vinh Tong,Andrei Manolache,Tanja Bien,Steffen Staab,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of discrete diffusion language models masks some response tokens and trains the model to recover their original values from the visible context. The masking pattern therefore determines both the context available to the model and the tokens it learns to predict. Uniform random masking does not explicitly account for the interaction between these choices. We introduce GoldiMask, which selects tokens to reveal as context by approximately maximizing a submodular objective. This objective uses model signals to balance the benefit of revealing tokens against their value as prediction targets. GoldiMask then weights the remaining targets according to how they benefit from the selected context and their remaining learning potential. Across three backbones and three training datasets, GoldiMask achieves the highest average accuracy in most evaluated settings, demonstrating gains on both reasoning and code generation. Component ablations show that both context selection and target weighting contribute to the gains. GoldiMask also reduces decoding iterations on GSM8K and MATH-500 under confidence-threshold parallel decoding, while maintaining comparable accuracy at higher confidence thresholds.

</details>


### [30] [Decode-Latency Feedback Prefill: A Model-Free Controller and Its Generalization Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38386)
*Gaurav Agarwal,Ashish Garg,Isha Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DLFP 是一种在 vLLM 中实施的、基于解码延迟反馈自适应调整 prefill 块的控制器，在小模型单 GPU 场景显著降低 P99 每令牌延迟，但由于调度信号局限性，不能直接推广到大模型或多 GPU；工作作为可复现的概念验证并指出需要以迭代完成时序为基础的控制器作为后续改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发自回归推理中，新的长提示( prompt ) 的预填充会阻塞正在解码的请求，造成延迟。固定预填充块大小可以减轻干扰但最优块大小依赖模型、硬件、负载和延迟目标，因此需要自适应控制。

Method: DLFP 在受保护的调度周期后，基于观测到的（解码期间）间隔作为比例反馈来调整接下来重叠活动解码的 prefill 块大小；对不与其他解码重叠的预填充不做限制。实现集成到 vLLM 中并在多种负载与测量管线下评估（Poisson 到达、精确令牌计数、真实请求轨迹、NVIDIA 遥测）。

Result: 在单 GPU（A100 80GB）上对 Qwen3-0.6B（BF16）进行三组 100 请求配对试验，P99 每令牌延迟分别降低 24.8%、30.1%、28.2%，均值 27.7%（配对 95% 置信区间 21.0%–34.3%），输出一致性无故障、SLO 合规不变；代价是平均 P99 首令牌时间上升 34.8%（仍在 SLO 内）。DLFP 未能在 Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-32B 及双 GPU 张量并行配置上带来收益，失败原因追溯到异步调度器调用间隔仅作为 GPU 完成迭代时间的代理信号。

Conclusion: 提出的 DLFP 是一种模型无关的控制器，能在并发自回归推理中减少已在解码的请求与新预填充(prefill)之间的干扰，从而显著降低 P99 每令牌延迟，但该机制的效用受调度器可观测信号限制，无法直接推广到更大模型或多 GPU 张量并行场景。
